SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Data.ball_subset {β : Fin 8 → ℚ}
  (d e : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Data β) (h : ∀ (j : Fin 9), |d.z j - e.z j| + d.r ≤ e.r) :
  Metric.closedBall d.toTube.center d.toTube.radius ⊆ Metric.closedBall e.toTube.center e.toTube.radius
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain.edge_strictMono {β : Fin 8 → ℚ} {n : ℕ}
  (s : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain β n) : StrictMono fun i => ↑(s.edge i)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain.piece_interval {β : Fin 8 → ℚ} {n : ℕ}
  (s : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain β n) (i : Fin (n + 1)) :
  (s.piece i).toTube.interval = Set.Icc ↑(s.edge i.castSucc) ↑(s.edge i.succ)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain.anchor_interval {β : Fin 8 → ℚ} {n : ℕ}
  (s : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain β n) (i : Fin (n + 2)) :
  (s.anchor i).toTube.interval = Set.Icc ↑(s.edge i) ↑(s.edge i)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain.ordered_overlap {β : Fin 8 → ℚ} {n : ℕ}
  (s : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain β n) {i j : Fin (n + 1)} {k : ℝ} (hij : i < j)
  (hi : k ∈ (s.piece i).toTube.interval) (hj : k ∈ (s.piece j).toTube.interval) :
  ∃ a,
    k ∈ a.interval ∧
      Metric.closedBall a.center a.radius ⊆ Metric.closedBall (s.piece i).toTube.center (s.piece i).toTube.radius ∧
        Metric.closedBall a.center a.radius ⊆ Metric.closedBall (s.piece j).toTube.center (s.piece j).toTube.radius
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChainCoverage.finite_interval_union {n : ℕ} (edge : Fin (n + 2) → ℝ)
  (hm : Monotone edge) : ⋃ i, Set.Icc (edge i.castSucc) (edge i.succ) = Set.Icc (edge 0) (edge (Fin.last (n + 1)))
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChainCoverage.chain_cover_domain {β : Fin 8 → ℚ} {n : ℕ}
  (s : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain β n) :
  s.cover.domain = Set.Icc ↑(s.edge 0) ↑(s.edge (Fin.last (n + 1)))
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChainCoverage.chain_whole_branch_smooth {β : Fin 8 → ℚ} {n : ℕ}
  (s : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain β n) :
  ContDiffOn ℝ (↑⊤) s.cover.root (Set.Icc ↑(s.edge 0) ↑(s.edge (Fin.last (n + 1))))
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChainCoverage.chain_whole_branch_ground {β : Fin 8 → ℚ} {n : ℕ}
  (s : SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelChain.Chain β n) {k : ℝ}
  (hk : k ∈ Set.Icc ↑(s.edge 0) ↑(s.edge (Fin.last (n + 1)))) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.normSq (s.cover.root k).1 = 1 ∧
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundBorder.groundPair
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.sourcePotential (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelTube.background β) k
          (s.cover.root k).1) =
      s.cover.root k

SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.increasing (i : Fin 286) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.edges i.castSucc <
    SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.edges i.succ
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.piece_bounds (i : Fin 286) :
  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.pieces i).a =
      SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.edges i.castSucc ∧
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.pieces i).c =
      SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.edges i.succ
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.anchor_bounds (i : Fin 287) :
  (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.anchors i).a =
      SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.edges i ∧
    (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.anchors i).c =
      SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.edges i
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.containment (i : Fin 286) (j : Fin 9) :
  |(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.anchors i.castSucc).z j -
            (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.pieces i).z j| +
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.anchors i.castSucc).r ≤
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.pieces i).r ∧
    |(SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.anchors i.succ).z j -
            (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.pieces i).z j| +
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.anchors i.succ).r ≤
      (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.pieces i).r
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.domain_eq :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.cover.domain = Set.Icc 0 30
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.whole_smooth :
  ContDiffOn ℝ (↑⊤) SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch (Set.Icc 0 30)
SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.whole_ground (κ : ℝ) (hk : κ ∈ Set.Icc 0 30) :
  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundRootCertificate.residual
        (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelTube.background SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalFirstTube.originalBackground)
        κ (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch κ) =
      0 ∧
    (∀ (i : Fin 8), 0 < (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch κ).1 i) ∧
      SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.normSq (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch κ).1 = 1 ∧
        SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundBorder.groundPair
              (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundState.sourcePotential
                (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelTube.background
                  SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalFirstTube.originalBackground)
                κ (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch κ).1) =
            SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch κ ∧
          SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundSourceJacobian.groundMap
              (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundKernelTube.background
                SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalFirstTube.originalBackground)
              κ (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch κ).1 =
            (SbtCosmo.Derivation.GroundOriginalContinuation.branch κ).1
